# ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.pde.runtime/icons/obj16/package_obj@2x.png) information
Information aims at defining the data transmission language (named Information due to the namespacing strange effects if it would have been named Data). It includes the notion of data as well as the different data communication means.

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *AbstractInstance*

Base class used to derive specific types of instances of classifiers (e.g very high-level/generic class)

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

Known Sub Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Part.gif) [Part](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-cs#-part), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/SystemFunction.gif) [SystemFunction](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-ctx#-systemfunction), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/ExchangeItemInstance.gif) [ExchangeItemInstance](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-information#-exchangeiteminstance), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/LogicalFunction.gif) [LogicalFunction](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-la#-logicalfunction), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/OperationalActivity.gif) [OperationalActivity](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-oa#-operationalactivity), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Role.gif) [Role](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-oa#-role), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/PhysicalFunction.gif) [PhysicalFunction](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-pa#-physicalfunction), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/SignalInstance.gif) [SignalInstance](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-communication#-signalinstance)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EEnum.gif) AggregationKind

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

defines the specific kind of a relationship, as per UML definitions

Common Types: 


|Attribute|Description|
|--|--|
|AGGREGATION|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#aggregation "An aggregation specifies a semantic relationship between a part and a whole. The part has a lifecycle of its own, and is potentially shared among several aggregatorsIndicates that the property has a shared aggregation.[source: UML superstructure v2.2]")
|ASSOCIATION|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#association "An association specifies a semantic relationship that can occur between typed instances. It has at least two endsrepresented by properties, each of which is connected to the type of the end. More than one end of the association mayhave the same type.[source: UML superstructure v2.2]Indicates that the property has no aggregation.[source: UML superstructure v2.2]")
|COMPOSITION|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#composition "A composition specifies a semantic relationship between whole and its parts. The parts lifecycles are tied to that of the whole, and they are not shared with any other aggregator.Indicates that the property is aggregated compositely, i.e., the composite object has responsibility for the existenceand storage of the composed objects.[source: UML superstructure v2.2]")
|UNSET|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#unset "used when value is not defined by the user")

A container for Association elements

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Association.gif) Association

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

An association specifies a semantic relationship that can occur between typed instances. It has at least two ends
represented by properties, each of which is connected to the type of the end. More than one end of the association may
have the same type.
An end property of an association that is owned by an end class or that is a navigable owned end of the association
indicates that the association is navigable from the opposite ends; otherwise, the association is not navigable from the
opposite ends.
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Class.gif) Class

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Collection.gif) Collection

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

A set of items of a given type.

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/CollectionValueReference.gif) CollectionValueReference

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

[source: Open Archival Information System (OAIS), IEC]

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

The relationship of a Part with something that it serves as a key for

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *MultiplicityElement*

A MultiplicityElement is an abstract metaclass that includes optional attributes for defining the bounds of a multiplicity.
A MultiplicityElement also includes specifications of whether the values in an instantiation of this element must be
unique or ordered.
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *Operation*

An operation is a behavioral feature of a classifier that specifies the name, type, parameters, and constraints for invoking
an associated behavior
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Parameter.gif) Parameter

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

A parameter is a specification of an argument used to pass information into or out of an invocation of a behavioral
feature.
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EEnum.gif) ParameterDirection

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

specifies the direction in which data is passed along through a parameter

Common Types: 


|Attribute|Description|
|--|--|
|EXCEPTION|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#exception "the parameter is like an exception")
|IN|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#in "used when the parameter represents an input of the operation it is used in")
|INOUT|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#inout "used when the parameter represents both an input and on output of the operation it is used in")
|OUT|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#out "used when the parameter represents an output of the operation it is used in")
|RETURN|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#return "used when the parameter represents the return value of the operation it is used in")
|UNSET|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#unset "used when the CommunicationLink protocol is not yet set")

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EEnum.gif) PassingMode

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

specifies the data passing mechanism for parameters of an operation

Common Types: 


|Attribute|Description|

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Property.gif) Property

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

A property is a structural feature.
A property related to a classifier by ownedAttribute represents an attribute, and it may also represent an association end.
It relates an instance of the class to a value or collection of values of the type of the attribute.
A property related to an Association by memberEnd or its specializations represents an end of the association. The type
of property is the type of the end of the association.
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EEnum.gif) SynchronismKind

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

specifies the synchronicity of an operation invocation

Common Types: 


|Attribute|Description|
|--|--|
|ASYNCHRONOUS|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#asynchronous "used to specify that the invocation of the operation is asynchronous, i.e. it is potentially completed before the actions performed in the operation are completed")
|SYNCHRONOUS|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#synchronous "used to specify that the invocation of the operation is synchronous, e.g. does not complete before the actions performed by the operation are complete")
|UNSET|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#unset "used when the synchronicity of the operation is not precised")

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Unit.gif) Unit

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

A Unit is a quantity in terms of which the magnitudes of other quantities that have the same dimension can be stated. A
unit often relies on precise and reproducible ways to measure the unit. For example, a unit of length such as meter may
be specified as a multiple of a particular wavelength of light. A unit may also specify less stable or precise ways to
express some value, such as a cost expressed in some currency, or a severity rating measured by a numerical scale
[source: SysML specification v1.1]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *Port*

A port is an interaction point between a block or part and its environment that
is connected with other ports via connectors
[source: SysML specification v1.1]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

Known Sub Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/PhysicalPort.gif) [PhysicalPort](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-cs#-physicalport), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/FunctionInputPort.gif) [FunctionInputPort](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-fa#-functioninputport), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/FunctionOutputPort.gif) [FunctionOutputPort](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-fa#-functionoutputport), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/ComponentPort.gif) [ComponentPort](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-fa#-componentport)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/PortRealization.gif) PortRealization

a PortRealization is a specific kind of allocation link between two Ports (typically of different design levels, or of different nature)

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/ExchangeItem.gif) ExchangeItem

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

Defined by functional characteristics that exist at a common boundary with co-functioning items and allow systems equipment to be compatible. 
An exchange item describes a required or produced data.
An exchange item has an exchange mechanism
[source:ARCADIA encyclopedia v0.8.0]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [Namespace](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namespace),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)
